## capitulation-exhaustion-reversal (intermediate) — 2026-04-11 [historical]
- **Works when:** Prior trend validated (price > 1h EMA200 within last 20 bars AND 4h EMA200 slope ≥ 0 in last 30 bars — Wyckoff SC requires preceding markup phase); speed gate (≥ 15% decline from 20-bar high in ≤ 5 bars — crash acceleration, not linear drift); ADX_4h < 40 (not entrenched downtrend); N ≥ 5 consecutive large-body red candles; volume > 3× SMA(20); RSI(14) < 20; MFI(14) < 12; Stoch < 10; price below 2.5σ BB; reversal candle (bullish close); **next-candle confirmation** (close > reversal_candle.high); BTC/USDT or ETH/USDT on 1h with 4h informative
- **Fails when:** No prior uptrend (structured downtrend — **#1 failure mode**; consecutive reds = trend continuation, not extinction burst; no trapped buyer cohort exists without prior markup); slow linear decline (< 15% in 5 bars = drift, mechanism absent); ADX_4h > 40 (entrenched bear — sellers are correct, not exhausting); next candle fails to close above reversal candle high (absorption rejected); volume spike with gap-down and no close recovery (distribution, not absorption); RSI < 20 sustained > 10 bars without bounce (structural freefall, not burst)
- **Best pair(s):** BTC/USDT, ETH/USDT (forced-liquidation events concentrated in high-liquidity pairs; institutional absorption most plausible)
- **Best timeframe:** 1h primary entry; 4h informative (ADX_4h, EMA200_4h slope)
- **Key numbers:** Glassnode n=7 BTC capitulation events 2017–2023: all 7 had prior uptrend, avg 30d return +31% (directional, not statistical); PMC9920669 regime-ungated RSI<30 = −97.5pp vs B&H (regime-gate analogy); next-candle confirmation: +5–10pp WR (sister prim meta); fixed vs trailing stop: avoids PF 2.0→0.603 collapse (EMA-pullback sister data); estimated signal frequency < 5/year on BTC 1h — statistical validation requires 3+ years minimum data
- **Critical findings:** (1) **Regime gate is the mechanism gate, not a refinement**: without prior uptrend, there is no trapped buyer cohort — the Wyckoff SC mechanism is void and the signal fires into trend continuation. (2) **Speed gate separates crash from drift**: Sornette & Johansen (2001) show genuine crashes have accelerating (super-exponential) decline; the ≥ 15% in ≤ 5 bars proxy is untuned but provides the qualitative separation. (3) **Single-tier consolidation is conservative**: extinction_burst_full (all conditions required) may produce < 5 signals/year; partial_burst and macro_capitulation tiers remain unvalidated and excluded. (4) **Glassnode n=7 is directional evidence only**: all 7 had prior uptrend + positive 30d return, but n is far below statistical inference threshold; the regime gate is Wyckoff-theoretical + analogy, not empirically derived from crypto data.
- **Evidence:** Wyckoff (1931) theoretical SC requirement; Murphy TAOFM (1999) SC definition; Elder (1993) volume climax; Glassnode (2023) n=7 BTC cap events; PMC9920669 regime-gate analogy; sister prim meta (stop + confirmation methodology)
- **Implementation gaps:** `YujiExtinctionBurstStrategy.py` needs: (1) regime gate `(close.shift(20) > ema_200_1h.shift(20)) & (ema_200_4h > ema_200_4h.shift(30))`, (2) speed gate `(close.shift(5) - close) / close.shift(5) >= 0.15`, (3) ADX_4h < 40 guard, (4) next-candle entry via `extinction_burst_signal.shift(1) & (close > close.shift(1))`, (5) fixed stop below wick low (remove trailing_stop=True), (6) drop `extinction_burst_partial` and `macro_capitulation` tiers until separately validated
- **Last validated:** never (intermediate refinement — own-data walk-forward on BTC/ETH 1h 2020–2025 is the blocking requirement; count regime-gated signals first; if n < 20, extend to multi-asset or mark frequency anti-prim)

## capitulation-exhaustion-reversal (sophisticated) — 2026-04-11
- **Works when:** Prior trend validated (price > 1h EMA200 within last 20 bars AND 4h EMA200 slope ≥ 0 in last 30 bars); **dual speed gate** (≥ 10% decline from 20-bar high in ≤ 5 bars [2022+ mature regime] OR ≥ 15% in ≤ 10 bars [legacy crash regime]); ADX_4h < 40 (not entrenched downtrend); N ≥ 5 consecutive large-body red candles; volume > 3× SMA(20); RSI(14) < 20; MFI(14) < 12; Stoch < 10; price below 2.5σ BB; reversal candle (bullish close); next-candle confirmation (close > reversal_candle.high); signal fires on **minimum 5-pair pool** (BTC, ETH + 3 major correlated liquid pairs) during panic event; position sized via `2% bankroll / |entry − wick_low|`; fee-adjusted R:R ≥ 1:3
- **Fails when:** No prior uptrend (**#1 failure mode** — consecutive reds = trend continuation, not extinction burst; Wyckoff SC mechanism absent without preceding markup); slow linear decline (< 10% in 5 bars = drift, speed gate not triggered); ADX_4h > 40 (entrenched bear — sellers are correct); next candle fails to close above reversal candle high (absorption rejected); BTC market maturation compresses crash amplitude below speed gate threshold (see structural risk); RSI < 20 sustained > 10 bars without bounce (structural freefall); MFI < 12 not reached (may reduce signal frequency further below 1-2/year)
- **Best pair(s):** BTC/USDT, ETH/USDT + correlated liquid majors (BNB, SOL, XRP) deployed simultaneously during panic events — multi-asset pool is statistically mandatory, not optional
- **Best timeframe:** 1h primary entry; 4h informative (ADX_4h, EMA200_4h slope)
- **Key numbers:** BTC crash frequency: ~0.9 cycle-level events/year (arxiv 2304.09939, 10+ events 2010-2021); capitulation wicks per crash: 1-3; BTC 1h qualifying signals: **1-2/year**; 5-pair pool: **5-10/year**; N_eff per panic (ρ≈0.90): **≈1.3 independent events**; need n_eff=50 = ~38 panic events = 4-8 years multi-asset deployment; WR margin at n=50: ±14% (95% CI); Glassnode n=7 30d return: +31%; crash amplitude trend: 87%→84%→77%→50% (declining); friction Sharpe erosion: ~47% (BSIC); OOS ceiling: 25-40% Sharpe loss; R:R ≥ 1:3 (wick stops are 20-30% wide)
- **Critical findings:** (1) **ZERO peer-reviewed crypto anchor at any tier** — no paper directly tests RSI < 20 + volume spike + consecutive reds + prior uptrend as combined signal on crypto. This is the ONLY sophisticated freqtrade prim with no positive quantitative anchor. (2) **Statistical floor is a binding constraint**: N_eff ≈ 1.3 per panic event due to ρ≈0.90 cross-asset correlation. Adding more pairs does NOT create independent observations — each market panic is fundamentally one event viewed across multiple instruments. n_eff=50 requires 38 genuine panic events; at 1-2/year it takes a generation on BTC alone. (3) **Market maturation structural risk**: Bitcoin crash amplitude declining per cycle (CoinDesk Apr 2026) — institutional ETF participation creates buy-the-dip floors that may prevent the speed gate from triggering in mature BTC. This is not a parameter calibration problem; it is a structural change in the asset. (4) **Dual speed gate design**: 10%/5 bars for 2022+ regime (compressed crashes); 15%/10 bars for legacy crash regime. Either qualifies — conservative design ensures the mechanism can fire in both the old and new crash character. Plateau test across both windows required. (5) **Dynamic position sizing is mandatory**: wick stops are 20-30% wide; fixed position size would over-risk on capitulation events. 2% bankroll / |entry − wick_low| normalizes risk per trade. (6) **Three anti-prim escape hatches formalized**: (A) frequency collapse: n < 15 in 5y multi-asset → maturation anti-prim; (B) plateau fails: no stable WR > 55% across 27-cell grid → mechanism anti-prim; (C) live WR < 48% over 30 events → efficiency anti-prim.
- **Evidence:** 8 sources — arxiv 2304.09939 (Bitcoin crisis enumeration), CoinDesk Apr 2026 (crash amplitude compression), PMC6599809 LPPLS (speed gate conceptual validation), MDPI 2021 bubbles/crashes detection, backtestbase.com (statistical power requirements), PMC9920669 (regime-gate analogy), BSIC (friction model), Wyckoff/Murphy (foundational SC mechanism)
- **Implementation gaps:** `YujiExtinctionBurstStrategy.py` needs 9 changes: (1) regime gate, (2) dual speed gate (`speed_fast ≥ 0.10/5bars | speed_slow ≥ 0.15/10bars`), (3) ADX_4h < 40 guard, (4) next-candle confirmation, (5) fixed stop at wick low (remove trailing_stop=True), (6) drop partial_burst and macro_capitulation tiers, (7) dynamic sizing `0.02 × bankroll / |entry − wick_low|`, (8) multi-asset whitelist (BTC+ETH+BNB+SOL+XRP), (9) dual speed gate plateau columns for hyperopt. Deployment blocked pending: (a) regime-gated signal count on BTC/ETH 1h 2020-2025 — if n < 15, frequency anti-prim triggered before plateau test can run; (b) if n ≥ 15, proceed to 27-cell plateau test with DSR correction; (c) own-data forward walk-forward across 5-pair pool.
- **Last validated:** never (ZERO peer-reviewed anchor; sophisticated tier means failure modes quantified and anti-prim escape hatches formalized; NOT that prim works — deployment blocked pending signal count and plateau test)

- **Evidence:** single academic source (Bawa, arxiv 2603.03152); zero own-data backtest

## funding-rate-crowding-reversal (intermediate) — 2026-04-11 [historical]
- **Works when:** BTC/ETH perpetual 8h funding rate > 0.10% per 8h (>109% annualized, top decile); ADX(14) < 45 (not parabolic-trend protected); RSI(14) > 60 (price has not yet corrected); crowding developed rapidly (< 3 days to spike); OI expanding with price (leveraged, not organic move); **suppresses long entries from all 6 sister prims for 72h window**. Inverse: funding < −0.05% → amplify long entry confidence.
- **Fails when:** Structural bull market sustained by new demand — 2020-2021 BTC: funding >0.10% for weeks while price continued up; naive contrarian loses repeatedly. Carry arbitrageurs exited market (2025 carry Sharpe negative) reducing the forced-exit mechanism. News-driven directional moves (fundamental buyers don't care about carry cost). Funding spike from single exchange anomaly.
- **Best pair(s):** BTC/USDT, ETH/USDT perpetual (reliable funding signal; altcoin funding noisy)
- **Best timeframe:** 8h funding candle resolution; 72h suppression window applied to 1h entry signals
- **Key numbers:** Top-decile funding (>0.10% per 8h) → ~60% 5-10% retrace within 72h (practitioner, 2024-2026, methodology undisclosed); carry Sharpe 2020-2025: **6.45** → **4.06 in 2024** → **negative in 2025** (arxiv 2510.14435); funding positive 92% of time (BitMEX Q3 2025 — baseline structural positive, extremes are the signal); CMU BTC carry Sharpe 12.8 and 7.0 (2020-2022); Jan 2026 BTC funding 0.51% per 8h = 70.2% APR paid by longs; signal frequency < 15-20 qualifying events/year on BTC+ETH combined.
- **Critical findings:** (1) **7th regime axis — DERIVATIVES CROWDING**: first prim in bank sourced from derivatives market (funding rate = perpetual position cost signal). All 6 prior prims are spot OHLCV-based. This extends the observational space to positioning/cost-of-carry. (2) **Meta-indicator role**: designed to modify confidence of sister prims rather than trigger independently. Architectural requirement for integration into existing strategy signal pipeline. (3) **Carry trade deterioration is not the same as crowding signal deterioration**: carry Sharpe went negative in 2025 because BASE RATE funding yield compressed (institutional entry, competition). But EXTREME funding events (>0.10%) still occur during directional rallies — these reflect crowding, not carry income. (4) **Primary quantitative claim unverified**: "60% / 72h" is a single practitioner source without disclosed methodology. No peer-reviewed paper tests funding rate extremes as directional price reversal signals directly. (5) **Trend persistence is the #1 failure mode and the reason for "intermediate" tier ceiling**: without a reliable regime gate that separates "parabolic bull funding persistence" from "overcrowded correction candidate," the signal will have significant false positive rate in bull markets. (6) **Freqtrade infrastructure gap**: requires futures data download; Binance changed from fixed 8h to variable intervals (GitHub issue #12583); current Yuji strategies are spot-mode — adding perp as informative pair is the implementation path. (7) **Signal frequency too low for rapid statistical validation**: <15-20 events/year means 2+ years needed for n=30; 4+ years for n=50. Statistical validation horizon is longer than for price-signal prims.
- **Evidence:** 9 sources — CMU Crypto Carry Trade (Sharpe 12.8, BTC Binance 2020-2022), arxiv 2510.14435 (Sharpe deterioration 2024-2025), BIS Working Paper 1087 (systematic factor), MDPI 2026 Two-Tiered Structure (17% arb events, CEX dominance), SSRN Inan (funding rate predictability), QuantJourney substack (sentiment signal), ScienceDirect (CEX/DEX arbitrage risk/return profiles), freqtrade GitHub #12583 (Binance interval variability), freqtrade GitHub #7302 (funding rate implementation)
- **Implementation gaps (8):** (1) Add `BTC/USDT:USDT` and `ETH/USDT:USDT` as informative pairs in each strategy's `informative_pairs()`, (2) `freqtrade download-data --trading-mode futures --pairs BTC/USDT:USDT ETH/USDT:USDT --timeframe 8h --candle-types funding_rate`, (3) Merge funding_rate column into 1h spot dataframe via `merge_informative_pair()`, (4) Handle Binance variable interval: if funding candle missing, use `fill_value=0.0` (conservative), (5) `funding_crowding = (df['BTC/USDT:USDT_8h_funding_rate'] > 0.0010).shift(1)` applied in `populate_indicators`, (6) Add `& ~funding_crowding` to all buy conditions in all 6 strategy files, (7) DecimalParameter `funding_extreme_high` (default 0.0010, range 0.0007-0.0020) for plateau test, (8) Plateau test: `funding_extreme_high ∈ [0.0007, 0.0010, 0.0015, 0.0020]` × `suppression_window_hours ∈ [24, 48, 72, 96, 168]` = 20-cell grid; PF variance < 25% required before elevation to sophisticated
- **Last validated:** never (NEW intermediate prim; first derivatives-market mechanism; BLOCKING infrastructure gap: futures data download and informative pair integration needed before any own-data validation; own-data backtest required before elevation; frequency anti-prim (A) trigger: if n < 15 qualifying events in 5-year BTC+ETH data)

## cross-venue-semantic-arb (naive) — 2026-04-11 [historical]

## cross-venue-semantic-arb (intermediate) — 2026-04-11
- **Works when:** Semantic Class 0 (identical resolution language, no Type A/B/C/D markers) or Class 1 (equivalent event, minor wording difference, no threshold/time divergence); gap > `(0.07 + PM_fee_rate) × p × (1−p) + 0.0215` for Class 0 or +2.5% additional buffer for Class 1; bilateral pre-funded accounts; cross-venue USD-equivalent depth ≥ 3× position size on BOTH legs; time-to-resolution > 6h; geopolitics × Kalshi political (floor 3.90% at p=0.50, 2.78% at p=0.10 — only viable systematic category); staged fill completes within 5s
- **Fails when:** Semantic Class 2 (Type A: quantitative threshold keywords; Type B: time window keywords "exceeding N hours", "consecutive", "continuous"; Type C: different documentation standard WH/NYT vs credible reporting; Type D: partial/full scope qualifier) — PRIMARY failure mode; gap ≤ dynamic friction floor `(0.07 + PM_fee_rate) × p × (1−p) + 0.0215`; politics/finance category (5.90% floor — requires rare 6%+ gap in semantic-risk zone); sports (5.40%), weather (6.65%), crypto (7.50%) — all require gaps in highest-semantic-risk territory; resolution < 6h (resolution-race window); less-liquid leg depth < 3× position (staged fill fails or over-slips); ZeroHash USDC↔USD conversion delay on Kalshi-side; leg 2 unfilled within 5s (directional binary exposure on leg 1)
- **Best pair(s):** Polymarket geopolitics (0% taker fee) × Kalshi political market (`0.07×P×(1−P)` taker); floor 3.90% at p=0.50 — narrowest viable window; tail brackets (p ≈ 0.10–0.20 or 0.80–0.90) lower friction to 2.78–3.30% but face higher semantic risk and adverse selection
- **Best timeframe:** Real-time WebSocket; staged fill target < 5s total; resolution > 6h; execute within 2s of gap detection (gap compression 4.5%→1.2% 2023→2025 confirms fast bot activity)
- **Key numbers:** Friction floor at p=0.50: geopolitics 3.90%, politics 5.90%, sports 5.40%, weather 6.65%, crypto 7.50%; tail (p=0.10) geopolitics: 2.78%; semantic Class 2 estimated skip rate 25–35%; Class 0/1 residual: 65–75% of co-listed events; 6% of 100k+ events co-listed (arxiv 2601.01706); practical viable universe ≈ 0.06 × 0.70 × [fraction with gap > floor] — highly filtered; 5-source evidence basis; zero own-data trades
- **Critical findings:** (1) **Dynamic friction floor resolves the paradox**: tail brackets (p=0.10) have lower friction (2.78% geopolitics) but higher semantic risk and thinner depth — these are simultaneously the best-friction and highest-semantic-risk events; Class 2 skip rate is highest in tail brackets. (2) **Semantic classifier estimated Class 2 rate 25–35%** is derived from 2-case taxonomy (shutdown, Bitcoin Reserve); actual rate unknown until a labeled co-listed event dataset is evaluated. (3) **Only geopolitics × Kalshi political is systematically viable as a taker strategy at p=0.50** — all other category combinations require gaps >5.4% which sit in the semantic-risk zone where tail deviations are most likely legitimate contract differences. (4) **Staged fill protocol does NOT eliminate directional exposure** — it reduces the window from "indefinite partial" to "0–5s naked binary" if leg 2 fails; still binary settlement risk during that window. (5) **Class 1 buffer of 2.5% is heuristic** — calibration against labeled divergence dataset is the blocking requirement for sophisticated elevation.
- **Evidence:** arxiv 2601.01706 (Gebele & Matthes, Jan 2026), Kalshi fee formula `0.07×P×(1−P)`, Polymarket category fee docs, newyorkcityservers.com 2026 guide, binary-arb-completeness sophisticated (fill protocol analogy)
- **Last validated:** never (intermediate elevation cycle 32; 0 own-data trades; viable window: Geopolitics × Kalshi Political, Class 0 only, gap > 4.5% [floor + 0.6% safety], resolution > 6h; blocking for sophisticated: labeled semantic dataset, dynamic slippage model, resolution-race classifier)

## cross-venue-semantic-arb (sophisticated) — 2026-04-11
- **Works when:** Semantic Class 0 (LLM embedding sim ≥ 0.92 AND no Type A/B/C/D/E keyword veto) or Class 1 (+2.5% buffer; +3.5% for Type D scope markers); gap > `(0.07 + PM_fee_rate) × p × (1−p) + 0.0215` + buffer; bilateral depth ≥ 3× position size on both legs; resolution horizon > 6h; geopolitics × Kalshi Political only (floor 3.90% at p=0.50); staged fill < 5s; gap > 4.5% (floor + 0.6% safety buffer); event NOT in government_shutdown / executive_order_conditional / mention-market-restricted categories (high Class 2 base rate)
- **Fails when:** Semantic Class 2 — any Type A (quantitative threshold), B (time window: "consecutive", "continuous", "exceeding N hours"), E (information source scope: "designated commentator" vs "any broadcast participant") veto → SKIP; Type D scope ("perform" vs "appear") without +3.5% buffer; gap ≤ friction floor (avg gap 2025 = 1.2% — below 3.90% floor on most events); Kalshi Rule 6.3(c) activated (ambiguous settlement at last price, not binary $1/$0 — eliminates expected gain without binary loss); leg 2 unfilled in 5s (directional binary exposure); ZeroHash USDC↔USD conversion delay; gap compressed below anti-prim threshold (rolling median < 2.5%)
- **Best pair(s):** Polymarket geopolitics (0% fee) × Kalshi political (0.07 taker coeff); no other category combination passes the friction floor at p=0.50 without entering high-semantic-risk territory
- **Best timeframe:** Real-time WebSocket; gap persistence 2–7 seconds; execute within 2s of detection; resolution > 6h
- **Key numbers:** Viable events: **3–8/year post-filter** (1,200–1,500 matched pairs/year; 25–35% Class 2 skip; avg gap 1.2% in 2025 — most below floor); gap persistence 2–7s; LLM classifier accuracy 60–70% (arxiv 2512.02436); Cardi B Rule 6.3(c) case: $57.3M volume ($47.3M Kalshi + $10M PM); Cardi B settlement YES=$0.26 / NO=$0.74 (not binary $1/$0); empirical divergence rate < 0.2% of co-listed events (3 documented in 3+ years); LA Mayoral reference gap: 7.53%; gap compression ~1.7pp/year (4.5% avg 2023 → 1.2% 2025)
- **Critical findings:** (1) **Cardi B Super Bowl (Feb 2026)** is the third documented divergence case — $47.3M Kalshi + $10M PM on one event; Kalshi invoked Rule 6.3(c) and settled at last price rather than binary resolution; Polymarket resolved YES at $1. Type D scope qualifier ("perform" vs "cameo/appear"). This case reveals that divergence risk for arb players is not always binary total loss — Kalshi may return capital at last price, which is LESS catastrophic but eliminates the expected gain. (2) **Rule 6.3(c) reformulates the loss model**: NOT always −$1 loss on wrong leg; Rule 6.3(c) returns $0.26 to YES holders and $0.74 to NO holders. If arb held YES on Kalshi and NO on Polymarket, Kalshi returned $0.26 (not $0), partially offsetting the Polymarket NO win. (3) **Type E classifier** (information source scope) extracted from NFL mention market analysis: Kalshi restricts resolution to "designated play-by-play and color commentators"; Polymarket resolves for any broadcast audio including field mics. Co-listed mention markets MUST check Type E markers or risk Class 2 divergence without classifier detection. (4) **LLM embedding classifier (arxiv 2512.02436)**: IBM+Columbia agentic AI for prediction market semantic clustering achieves 60–70% accuracy; validates embedding over keyword for borderline cases; hybrid (embedding + Type A/B/C/D/E keyword veto) should exceed 70% and eliminate the known error types. (5) **Viable universe is time-constrained**: gap compression ~1.7pp/year; at this rate, geopolitics gaps average below friction floor by ~2027 for liquid markets; new market listings create temporary windows. The edge is not scalable — it is early-listing arbitrage, not systematic. (6) **Anti-prim B (Rule 6.3(c) rate)**: if Kalshi routinely invokes Rule 6.3(c) on > 15% of co-listed trades, regulatory ambiguity has structurally disrupted the binary settlement assumption that makes arb possible — signal type changes from arbitrage to speculation.
- **Evidence:** 9 sources — arxiv 2601.01706 (Gebele & Matthes, semantic non-fungibility, Jan 2026), arxiv 2512.02436 (IBM+Columbia semantic trading, Dec 2025), Fortune (Cardi B divergence, Feb 2026), CBS News (Cardi B resolution dispute), DeFiRate (Kalshi Rule 6.3(c)), AhaSignals (1,200–1,500 matched pairs, gap persistence 2–7s), Monad Blog (prediction market divergence), newyorkcityservers.com 2026 arb guide, laikalabs.ai (LA Mayoral 7.53% gap case)
- **Anti-prim triggers:** (A) rolling 30-day median viable gap on geopolitics × Kalshi < 2.5%; (B) Kalshi Rule 6.3(c) activation rate > 15% of own-data trades; (C) 2+ semantic divergence losses in 12 months despite Class 2 filter
- **Implementation gaps:** `src/classifiers/semantic_risk.py`: (1) sentence-transformer embedding on resolution texts; (2) Type A/B/C/D/E keyword veto as override; (3) BLOCKING: calibrate embedding thresholds against labeled corpus ≥ 50 events. `src/strategies/cross_venue_arb.py`: (4) `CLASS_1_TYPE_D_EXTRA = 0.010` parameter; (5) gap tracker for anti-prim (A) monitoring; (6) dynamic slippage model replacing 2% flat. All from bilateral_fill.py (intermediate) unchanged.
- **Last validated:** never (sophisticated elevation cycle 33; 0 own-data trades; BLOCKING: labeled corpus ≥ 50 co-listed events with known resolution outcomes required before LLM classifier deployment; anti-prim circuit-breakers deployed from first trade)

## semantic-correlation-pair-trade (intermediate) — 2026-04-11 [historical]
- **Works when:** 4-class logical relationship confirmed (Class 1: Implication; Class 2: Positive Correlation; Class 3: Temporal Chain; Class 4: Anti-correlation, non-exhaustive); sentence-transformer embedding similarity ≥ 0.82; implied price constraint violated by > `fee_rate×p(1−p)+0.015+0.03` (geopolitics: 4.5%; politics: 5.5%; sports: 5.25%; weather: 5.75%); both markets liquid ≥ $5k; resolution horizons within 14 days; no Type B (time window) oracle divergence; NOT exhaustive-sum pair (binary-arb-completeness handles those); no asymmetric scheduled news catalyst; position sized via fractional-kelly-sizing sophisticated with correlated N_eff (ρ ≈ 0.60–0.85 for causally linked markets → N_eff ≈ 1.2–1.5 for 2 positions)
- **Fails when:** Oracle criterion divergence within Polymarket (same topic, different thresholds — e.g., "3 rate cuts" vs "5 rate cuts" — correlation exists but the price relationship isn't violated, it's correctly distinct); asymmetric new information breaks correlation (the divergence IS the new information, not a misprice — OBI signal at movement time can help classify); lagging market too illiquid for convergence before resolution; resolution horizon divergence > 14 days (legitimate temporal discount); exhaustive-sum pair with ΣP < 0.99 (binary-arb-completeness is guaranteed profit, not probabilistic); relationship is narrative-similar but causally independent (e.g., two different countries' elections with no shared driver)
- **Best pairs:** Class 1 implication pairs and Class 3 temporal chain pairs on geopolitics category (0% fee — lowest friction floor at 4.5%); political event clusters where causal chains are well-defined
- **Best timeframe:** Days-to-weeks holding horizon; daily convergence check; max 14-day hold per position
- **Key numbers:** ~20% average return per week-long horizon (arxiv 2512.02436 simulation); 60–70% relationship classification accuracy (same paper); friction floor geopolitics p=0.50: 4.5%; politics p=0.50: 5.5%; correlation for same-topic market pairs ρ ≈ 0.60–0.85; zero own-data trades
- **Critical findings:** (1) **Structural advantage over cross-venue arb**: single venue, no bilateral capital, no cross-chain execution risk, no Rule 6.3(c) ambiguous settlement risk. Friction floor (4.5%) comparable to cross-venue geopolitics (3.90%) but without the execution infrastructure risk. (2) **Convergence is probabilistic, not contractual**: unlike binary-arb-completeness where ΣP = $1 is enforced at resolution, this prim relies on market participants discovering and trading the inconsistency. No convergence guarantee. (3) **Class 3 temporal chain (nested-horizon markets) is mechanistically the cleanest**: P(event by Q3) ≥ P(event by Q1) is a mathematical constraint; when violated it is unambiguous (not a correlation estimate, not a prior-dependent inference). Should be the first backtest target. (4) **Resolution oracle risk is same-venue type**: the primary failure mode is not platform divergence (same venue) but criterion-specification divergence within Polymarket (one contract "any 3 cuts" another "at least 5 cuts" — related but NOT the same constraint). Type A/D veto from semantic_risk.py handles this. (5) **Portfolio correlation adjustment is mandatory**: two positions in the same causal cluster have ρ ≈ 0.80 → N_eff ≈ 1.1 → effectively one position; Kelly sizing must reflect this via fractional-kelly-sizing sophisticated N_eff formula.
- **Evidence:** arxiv 2512.02436 (IBM+Columbia, Dec 2025), Polymarket fee documentation, cross-venue-semantic-arb sophisticated (oracle risk taxonomy), fractional-kelly-sizing sophisticated (correlated N_eff)
- **Last validated:** never (7th polymarket prim; no implementation; BLOCKING before deployment: 20 Class 1 implication pairs with known resolution outcomes to calibrate P(B|A) priors and validate 60–70% classifier accuracy on Polymarket market text) [historical — superseded by sophisticated]

## semantic-correlation-pair-trade (sophisticated) — 2026-04-11
- **Works when:** Granger causality confirms lead-market direction AND LLM semantic stage approves economic transmission mechanism; sentence-transformer embedding ≥ 0.82; 4-class relationship active — **Class 3 Temporal Chain** (P(Tn) ≥ P(T1), deterministic math constraint, near-zero downside, no prior needed) OR **Class 1 Implication** (P(B) ≥ P(A)·P(B|A), requires prior calibration) OR **Class 2 Positive Correlation** (Granger+LLM mandatory, highest downside) OR **Class 4 Anti-correlation** (ΣP(A)+P(B)+ε < $1); constraint violation > dynamic friction floor (geopolitics: 4.5% at p=0.50; politics: 5.5%); both markets liquid ≥ $5k; resolution horizons within 14 days; no Type A/B oracle veto; NOT exhaustive-sum (binary-arb handles those); Bregman/KL-divergence position sizing applied; N_eff ≈ 1.1 enforced for same-event cluster; 14-day hard stop OR convergence-to-<1.5% exit
- **Fails when:** Convergence not guaranteed — divergence widens or never closes before resolution (primary failure; 2–4% persistent mispricing means some gaps are structurally permanent, not mispricings); **Granger leads are spurious** (short time-series; LLM re-rank mandatory, not optional — spurious Granger leads without LLM filter = Class 2 primary failure); asymmetric news catalyst breaks correlation (divergence IS new information, not misprice; OBI signal as partial classifier); lagging market illiquidity (position size constrained to depth_lagging × 0.80; deep gaps can be > market can absorb); resolution horizons diverge > 14 days (legitimate temporal discount, not misprice); Class 1 without P(B|A) prior calibration (wrong prior → false signal magnitude); exhaustive-sum pairs (binary-arb-completeness is correct prim — contractual guarantee vs probabilistic); rollover — holding past one market's resolution creates naked single-market exposure
- **Best markets:** Class 3 Temporal Chain geopolitics pairs (0% fee, deterministic constraint) — e.g., "event by Q1 2026" vs "event by Q3 2026" (same underlying, longer horizon must price ≥ shorter); Class 1 Implication pairs within same election event cluster (candidate X wins primary → candidate X wins general)
- **Best timeframe:** Class 3: execute within minutes of constraint violation detection; Class 1/2: 7-day median hold (2602.07048); hard exit at 14 days regardless of position
- **Key numbers:** 7,000+ markets with combinatorial mispricings over 12 months (2508.03474); $40M profits extracted (combined intra+inter); 13 logical pairs in 2024 US election, 5 profitable; hybrid PnL doubles vs Granger alone at 7-day horizon (2602.07048, 18 evaluations); LLM detection accuracy 60–70% (2512.02436); 2–4% persistent mispricing in liquid markets (Moontower); 75% of mechanical arb orders execute within 1 hour; probabilistic trades hold 7-day median; N_eff ≈ 1.1 for same-event ρ≈0.80; geopolitics friction floor 4.5%; Class 3 downside ≈ 0; Class 2 downside highest
- **Critical findings:** (1) **Real extraction evidence confirms mechanism**: 7,000+ combinatorial mispricings and $40M extracted (2508.03474) converts intermediate's simulation-only anchor to empirically grounded. (2) **Granger+LLM hybrid is not optional** — pure static classification (intermediate) exposes Class 2 pairs to fragile correlations that break silently; LLM semantic re-rank specifically filters these and doubles PnL by reducing downside events, not increasing WR. The WR improvement is NOT the mechanism; downside frequency reduction IS. (3) **Class 3 Temporal Chain is the beachhead**: P(Tn) ≥ P(T1) is deterministic; no prior calibration required; near-zero downside (constraint cannot invert); mechanically the cleanest first own-data target. (4) **Bregman/KL divergence = correct position sizing**: max profit per pair = KL distance from current joint price distribution to no-arbitrage polytope; Frank-Wolfe iterative algorithm handles multi-pair portfolio; this is the mathematical architecture used by the $40M extraction bots. (5) **N_eff ≈ 1.1 for same-event cluster** is binding constraint: two correlated positions in same event have ρ ≈ 0.80 → effectively one position; Kelly must enforce this or overcapitalization on correlated losses. (6) **"75% within 1 hour" is for mechanical arb, not correlation trade**: the intermediate implied fast convergence; the probabilistic correlation hold is 7-day median (2602.07048); the 14-day hard stop is the appropriate exit horizon.
- **Evidence:** 7 sources — arxiv 2508.03474 (IMDEA AFT 2025, 7,000+ mispricings, $40M, Bregman framework), arxiv 2602.07048 (LLM Risk Manager Feb 2026, Granger+LLM hybrid doubles PnL), arxiv 2512.02436 (IBM+Columbia Dec 2025, 60–70% accuracy, 4-class taxonomy), Rootdata 2026 (Frank-Wolfe / KL divergence mathematical infrastructure), Moontower (2–4% persistent mispricing; temporal chain option-chain analogy), binary-arb-completeness sophisticated (hold horizon analogy), fractional-kelly-sizing sophisticated (N_eff formula)
- **Anti-prim triggers:** (A) rolling 20-trade mean net edge < 4.5% (geopolitics floor) → HFT absorbed logical arbitrage; (B) correlation breakdown rate > 25% → LLM classifier structural failure; (C) convergence failure rate > 40% within 14-day windows → probabilistic convergence mechanism absent
- **Implementation gaps (8):** (1) `src/strategies/correlated_pair_trade.py` — scanner + entry/exit; (2) `src/classifiers/relationship_type.py` — 4-class + oracle veto; (3) `src/classifiers/lead_lag.py` — Granger + LLM semantic re-rank [BLOCKING for Class 2]; (4) `src/risk/bregman.py` — KL distance to no-arb polytope via Frank-Wolfe [BLOCKING for sizing]; (5) asymmetric stop logic by class; (6) N_eff register for correlated position pairs; (7) three anti-prim circuit-breakers; (8) Class 3 own-data scanner as first validation target (count violations ≥ 4.5% in 3 months; if < 10, frequency anti-prim precursor)
- **Last validated:** never (sophisticated elevation cycle 35; 0 own-data trades; Class 3 Temporal Chain is first deployment target — deterministic constraint, no prior, no Granger needed, beachhead for anti-prim validation)

## financial-market-lead-lag (naive) — 2026-04-11
- **Works when:** CME FedWatch probability shifts significantly after an economic data release (CPI, NFP, PCE, FOMC minutes, Fed speeches); Polymarket equivalent market has NOT yet adjusted; gross gap ≥ 6%; PM question resolves as a binary on a specific FOMC meeting date (direct 1:1 CME contract mapping); CME data is fresh (< 5 minutes old); time-to-PM-resolution > 2h
- **Fails when:** Resolution criteria diverge — PM question is multi-meeting ("at least one cut in 2026"), magnitude-conditional ("50bps cut"), or conditional-timing ("cut before June") and does not map to a single FOMC meeting CME contract; gap < friction floor (PM politics ~4% fee → net floor ~3%); PM market already efficient (institutional participants have automated pipelines from same data); CME surprise error (emergency cut, inter-meeting move — PM social-media crowd may actually be faster on extreme events); lag window compressed below execution threshold (sub-5 minute in 2025+ for liquid Fed markets)
- **Best pair(s):** Polymarket "Will Fed cut/raise at [specific FOMC meeting date]?" — binary resolution, direct mapping to CME FedWatch meeting-specific probability
- **Best timeframe:** Real-time; execute within 5–30 minutes of economic data release (CPI 8:30 ET, NFP 8:30 ET, PCE 8:30 ET, FOMC announcement 2:00 ET); window estimates: 2023 ~hours → 2025 ~30 minutes → likely narrowing further
- **Key numbers:** 0 own trades; mechanism: CME vol $billions (institutional) vs PM vol $millions (retail); Gürkaynak et al. (2005 AER): fed funds futures update to policy information in sub-minutes at institutional desks; lag is retail processing time, not information unavailability
- **Critical findings:** (1) **8th prim class** — first PM prim using external traditional financial market data as leading indicator. (2) **Resolution criteria mapping is the #1 failure mode and implementation blocker** — no automated classifier exists for PM question → CME contract equivalence. (3) **Time-bounded edge** — institutional PM participation is growing; lag window compressing year-over-year; viable window may be years not decades. (4) **Mechanism is information hierarchy, not contractual** — unlike binary-arb, there is no guaranteed convergence; CME can be wrong (low probability, documented cases).
- **Evidence:** paper (adjacent financial economics, Gürkaynak 2005, Snowberg 2007); hypothesis for cross-market PM application; zero own-data
- **Implementation gaps:** (1) CME FedWatch data feed (`src/signals/cme_fedwatch.py`), (2) PM Fed-market detector (Gamma API filter by "Federal Reserve"), (3) resolution-criteria classifier (PM question → CME meeting mapping), (4) freshness guard (CME data < 5min), (5) fractional-kelly-sizing integration at α=0.10
- **Last validated:** never (NEW naive prim, cycle 36 RESEARCH; first external-institutional-market signal class; no implementation; BLOCKING: resolution-criteria classifier required before any trade can be entered safely)

## financial-market-lead-lag (intermediate) — 2026-04-11
- **Works when:** **Mode A (event-driven)**: CPI/NFP/PCE within past 30 min (or FOMC announcement within 15 min) AND CME FedWatch updates ≥ 5pp directional shift on specific FOMC meeting contract AND equivalent PM market lags AND bid-ask ≥ 2% (not bot-competed) AND gap ≥ 6% — enter in CME direction. **Mode B (persistent drift)**: CME FedWatch has directionally led PM for ≥ 3 consecutive trading days AND gap ≥ 8% AND bid-ask ≥ 3% AND no scheduled data release within 48h — enter in CME direction. Both modes: single-meeting binary PM resolution (specific FOMC date, ≥25bp bracket); liquidity ≥ $10k; resolution > 2h; α=0.10 Kelly floor (mandatory — no calibration yet)
- **Fails when:** **Resolution criteria mismatch (#1 failure mode)** — multi-meeting ("at least one cut in 2026"), multi-magnitude ("50bps by Q2"), conditional-timing PM questions don't map to single CME meeting contracts; **bid-ask < threshold** (Mode A: < 2%; Mode B: < 3%) — sub-100ms bots have already priced parity (2.52c/contract execution advantage, 73% of PM arb extracted by bots); **no data release context for Mode A** (gap without trigger event = no lag mechanism); **emergency/inter-meeting FOMC** (PM social-media crowd may update faster than CME on extreme events); gap persists > 72h without narrowing (structural pricing difference, not lag); rounding rule edge case (12.5bp unscheduled move breaks 25bp bracket mapping)
- **Best pair(s):** Polymarket "Will Fed cut/hold/raise at [specific FOMC meeting]?" markets with single-date, ≥25bp bracket binary resolution
- **Best timeframe:** Mode A: execute within 5–30 min of economic data release (CPI/NFP/PCE 8:30 ET; FOMC 2:00 ET); Mode B: daily check vs CME FedWatch rolling
- **Key numbers:** CME leads Polymarket **63.4% of FOMC cycles** (Medium FedWatch vs Polymarket analysis); average PM lag **~1.44 days** across full cycle; bot execution advantage **2.52c/contract** (Della Vedova SSRN 6191618); bots extract **73% of PM arb profits** (sub-100ms); **70% of PM users unprofitable** (execution quality dominant); Mode A net edge post-fee: 2–3% (thin margin); Mode B net edge: 4% (higher gap compensates for execution risk)
- **Critical findings:** (1) **Execution quality is the binding constraint, not information quality** (Della Vedova): CME→PM directional signal is real (63.4% CME leads) but sub-100ms bots capture 73% of arb profits; winning requires execution speed or accessing windows bots don't compete in (Mode B drift). (2) **1.44-day average lag is full-cycle average**: driven by periods of secular drift, not just post-release spikes; Mode B targets this. (3) **Resolution criteria taxonomy defined**: single-meeting binary (≥25bp bracket, official FOMC statement) = clean; rounding rule exception for 12.5bp is low-probability but must be handled. (4) **Source quality gap**: primary quantitative anchor (63.4%, 1.44 days) is Medium article, not peer-reviewed; treat as practitioner hypothesis, not evidence. (5) **Lag is time-bounded**: trajectory from hours (2023) to minutes (2025); Mode A actionable window compressing toward sub-5min by 2027 for liquid Fed markets.
- **Evidence:** 6 sources — Medium/PolymarketNow (63.4% CME leads, 1.44-day lag), Gürkaynak/Sack/Swanson 2005 AER (fed funds sub-minute update), Snowberg/Wolfers/Zitzewitz 2007 AER P&P (cross-market co-pricing), Della Vedova SSRN 6191618 (70% unprofitable, 2.52c/contract bot advantage), Reichenbach & Walther SSRN 5910522 (PM prices track realized probabilities), arxiv 2508.03474 (73% arb by sub-100ms bots)
- **Implementation gaps (5):** (1) `src/signals/cme_fedwatch.py` — CME API feed for meeting-specific probabilities, (2) `src/strategies/financial_lead_lag.py` — resolution-criteria classifier (single-meeting binary detector), two-mode gap tracker with trigger windows, (3) data release calendar integration (CPI/NFP/PCE/FOMC dates + consensus estimates), (4) bid-ask spread computation per market (CLOB WebSocket), (5) anti-prim circuit-breakers (A) gap compression monitor and (B) WR tracker
- **Last validated:** never (intermediate elevation cycle 37; zero own trades; 5 implementation gaps all blocking; Mode B frequency on BTC/ETH historical data equivalent unknown; anti-prim at gap < 4% avg over 20 events or WR < 55% / 30 trades) [historical — superseded by sophisticated]

## financial-market-lead-lag (sophisticated) — 2026-04-11
- **Works when:** **Mode A (second-wave)**: CPI/NFP/PCE release within past 30 min (or FOMC within 15 min) AND CME FedWatch updates ≥5pp on specific FOMC meeting contract AND PM market lags AND bid-ask ≥ 2% AND gap ≥ 6% — **enter at T+5 to T+30 min only** (skip first 5 min Tier 1 HFT burst). **Mode B (persistent drift)**: CME FedWatch has led PM ≥ 3 consecutive trading days AND gap ≥ 8% AND bid-ask ≥ 3% AND no data release within 48h. Both: single-meeting binary PM (specific FOMC date, ≥25bp bracket); liquidity ≥ $10k; resolution > 2h; α=0.10 Kelly floor; **fomc_pm_mapper.py classifier BLOCKING**
- **Fails when:** Resolution criteria mismatch (#1 failure mode — multi-meeting/multi-magnitude/conditional-timing PM questions); bid-ask below efficiency gate (bots competed); emergency/inter-meeting FOMC (PM may update faster than CME); gap persists > 72h without narrowing (structural, not lag); Mode A entered before T+5 (competing with Tier 1 HFT burst); Mode B cancelled by imminent data release; rounding-rule edge case (12.5bp unscheduled cut breaks 25bp bracket)
- **Best pair(s):** Polymarket "Will Fed cut/hold/raise at [specific FOMC meeting date]?" — single meeting, ≥25bp bracket, official FOMC statement as oracle
- **Best timeframe:** Mode A: T+5 to T+30 min post CPI/NFP/PCE (8:30 ET) or FOMC (2:00 ET); Mode B: daily vs CME FedWatch rolling
- **Key numbers:** CME leads PM **63.4% of FOMC cycles** (practitioner, Medium); avg PM lag **~1.44 days** (practitioner, Medium); bot arb share **73%** (arxiv 2508.03474); bot execution advantage **2.52c/contract** (Della Vedova SSRN 6191618); 70% PM users unprofitable; Mode A frequency **2–4/year**; Mode B frequency **2–3/year**; combined **4–7/year**; N=30 requires **4–8 years**; WR margin at N=30 **±18%**; breakeven WR at 4% fee **52%**; safe target **57%**; Mode A net edge post-fee ~2–3%; Mode B ~4%
- **Critical findings:** (1) **Stratified 3-tier competitive model**: Tier 1 <100ms bots (mechanical arb, 73% of PM profits — NOT Mode A target); Tier 2 5–30 min second-wave (Mode A window — human macro-interpretation delay); Tier 3 1–3 days (Mode B window — retail attention allocation lag). Mode A targets Tier 2, not Tier 1. (2) **Kuttner (2001, JME) anchor**: fed funds futures absorb monetary policy surprises efficiently; CME FedWatch is the canonical "correct" probability; PM deviation = measurable retail lag, not disagreement. (3) **Wolfers & Zitzewitz (2004, JEP)**: PM slower to incorporate complex financial/economic information than political events — specific mechanism for why CPI/NFP interpretation creates Mode A lag (financial expertise required). (4) **Source quality ceiling permanently acknowledged**: mechanism is 4-paper academic (Kuttner, Gürkaynak, Snowberg, Wolfers & Zitzewitz); PM-specific magnitude (63.4%, 1.44d) is practitioner-only; sophisticated tier carries this caveat — mechanism proven, magnitude unvalidated. (5) **Frequency is FOMC-calendar-constrained**: adding more PM pairs doesn't increase signal rate; there is one Federal Reserve; n=30 requires 4–8 years. (6) **Anti-prim C formalized**: if Fed enters steady-state policy and Mode A yields < 2 events/year for ≥ 3 consecutive years → frequency anti-prim (not a parameter problem). (7) **BLOCKING classifier** remains the zero-trade gate: fomc_pm_mapper.py classifier must be implemented and validated against ≥ 20 historically resolved PM Fed questions before any live trade.
- **Evidence:** 8 sources — Kuttner (2001, JME), Wolfers & Zitzewitz (2004, JEP), Gürkaynak/Sack/Swanson (2005, AER), Snowberg/Wolfers/Zitzewitz (2007, AER P&P), Della Vedova (SSRN 6191618), Reichenbach & Walther (SSRN 5910522), arxiv 2508.03474 (IMDEA AFT 2025), Medium/PolymarketNow (practitioner magnitude source)
- **Implementation gaps (5 — all BLOCKING for Mode A; gaps 3–5 block Mode B):** (1) `src/signals/cme_fedwatch.py` — CME API feed + freshness gate, (2) `src/classifiers/fomc_pm_mapper.py` — **BLOCKING**: resolution-criteria classifier validated against ≥ 20 labeled historical questions, (3) `src/strategies/financial_lead_lag.py` — two-mode signal logic with T+5/T+30 window + Mode B 3-day counter, (4) `src/risk/antiprims.py` — three escape hatch circuit-breakers, (5) `src/models/kelly_sizing.py` — α=0.10 mandatory until N_eff ≥ 30
- **Last validated:** never (sophisticated elevation cycle 38; ZERO own-data trades; mechanism 4-paper academic; PM-specific magnitude practitioner-only; BLOCKING: fomc_pm_mapper.py classifier required before first trade; anti-prim (A): rolling 20 Mode A events gap < 4%; anti-prim (B): WR < 52% / 30 trades; anti-prim (C): < 2 Mode A events/year for 3 years)

- **Works when:** Divergence ≥ 8% between Metaculus community median (or GJP consensus) and Polymarket YES price; semantically equivalent question confirmed (same oracle, same threshold, same scope); PM liquidity ≥ $5k; resolution horizon 3–90 days; geopolitics/politics category (0–4% fee); calibrated venue has ≥ 100 predictors

No Polymarket-specific cross-venue calibration data yet.

## superforecaster-consensus-lead (intermediate) — 2026-04-11
- **Works when:** **Mode A**: Metaculus divergence ≥ 10% from PM YES (≤ 48h old, Metaculus last update < 24h, ≥ 150 predictors, PM bid-ask ≥ 3%, resolution 7–30 days, geopolitics only). **Mode B**: divergence ≥ 8% persisted ≥ 72h (≥ 100 predictors, PM bid-ask ≥ 2%, resolution 30–90 days, geopolitics + politics/finance). Both modes: PM $5k–$50k; semantically equivalent question (manual review; metaculus_pm_matcher.py BLOCKING); α=0.10 Kelly floor
- **Fails when:** Semantic non-fungibility **#1 failure mode** — different resolution criteria across platforms; resolution horizon < 7 days (**PM advantage at < 7 days**, Wiley 2012 — this prim inverts); PM > $50k (sophisticated participants already priced in calibration advantage); breaking news within 6h (PM updates faster than superforecaster cycle); Metaculus consensus stale (> 24h since update); geopolitics groupthink on high-profile polarised events; question outside OECD geopolitics domain
- **Best pair(s):** Geopolitical events (elections, conflict outcomes, diplomatic negotiations) and politics/finance (Mode B only). Avoid: sports (PM advantage), weather (ensemble-forecast-edge prim covers), science/AI (Metaculus speciality but no PM match)
- **Best timeframe:** Mode A: 7–30 days to resolution; Mode B: 30–90 days. Entry: as soon as divergence and staleness thresholds met. Hold: until resolution or divergence < 4% (convergence)
- **Key numbers:** Metaculus Brier score **0.111** vs Polymarket Brier **0.187** (~40% accuracy gap, Manifund); GJP vs IC prediction markets: **−30% Brier** (Tetlock & Mellers 2015); GJP vs futures markets (FOMC 2024): **+30% outperformance** (Good Judgment); breakeven WR at 4% fee: **52%**; safe target: **57%**; realistic WR estimate: **55–65%** (hypothesis — no own-data); Signal frequency: **unknown** — frequency scan on Metaculus-Polymarket divergence is a pre-deployment requirement
- **Critical findings:** (1) **Mechanism is calibration-based convergence, NOT time-lag** — distinct from financial-market-lead-lag. Superforecasters don't have faster information access; they have better probability estimates at same information level. Convergence requires PM participants to discover the gap (slower). (2) **Brier gap is quantified (first time)**: Metaculus 0.111 vs PM 0.187 on matched events. This is a 40% absolute accuracy advantage, not a relative tournament result. The trading signal is grounded in a measurable accuracy differential. (3) **7-day horizon floor is non-negotiable**: Wiley 2012 explicitly documents PM accuracy exceeds expert consensus at sub-7-day horizons (financially-incentivised traders outperform calibration-incentivised ones when resolution is imminent). (4) **GJP vs futures (FOMC, 2024) confirms cross-domain validity**: calibration advantage extends beyond geopolitics into monetary policy — domain is broader than previously assumed but not universal. (5) **No direct WR study exists** for Metaculus divergence → PM resolution outcome. The 55–65% target is derived from the Brier gap by analogy, not measured. This prim remains in the hypothesis tier until own-data.
- **Evidence:** 8 sources — Tetlock & Mellers 2015 Psych Sci, Mellers et al. 2015 Psych Sci, Manifund/Medium Metaculus vs Polymarket Brier comparison, Atanasov et al. SSRN 4691513 2024 (Crowd Prediction Systems), Wiley 2012 calibration study, Good Judgment 2024 FOMC outperformance, Reichenbach & Walther SSRN 5910522, Snowberg & Wolfers 2004 JEP
- **Implementation gaps (5):** (1) `src/classifiers/metaculus_pm_matcher.py` — BLOCKING; (2) `src/signals/metaculus_feed.py` — API integration; (3) `src/strategies/superforecaster_lead.py` — Mode A/B logic; (4) `src/risk/antiprims.py` — escape hatch circuit-breakers; (5) predictor count quality filter
- **Last validated:** never (cycle 40 RESEARCH; intermediate elevation; 8-source basis; no own-data; BLOCKING: metaculus_pm_matcher.py before any trade; anti-prim (A): rolling 20 events WR < 55%; anti-prim (B): own-data 30 geopolitics trades WR < 52%)

## superforecaster-consensus-lead (sophisticated) — 2026-04-11
- **Works when:** Semantic equivalence confirmed via `metaculus_pm_matcher.py`; source-quality-tiered divergence: GJP/Almanis ≥ 8% / Metaculus tracked ≥ 10% (≥15% tracked status in question community) / community median ≥ 12% (≥200 predictors); horizon **14–45 days** (Mode A peak zone); PM $5k–$50k; bid-ask ≥ 3% (Mode A) or ≥ 2% (Mode B); geopolitics category (Mode A); no breaking news within 12h; divergence ≤ 48h old; Metaculus updated < 24h
- **Fails when:** Semantic mismatch (#1 — no automated gate until matcher built); **T < 7 days unconditionally** (PM outperforms — financial incentives dominate); PM > $50k (sophisticated PM participants already priced in calibration advantage); community-median source with flat 8% threshold (unweighted community ties markets, not beats — Atanasov 2024; use ≥ 12% threshold only); breaking news (PM updates faster than Metaculus survey cycle); groupthink on high-profile polarised events; non-OECD geopolitics (calibration advantage domain uncertain)
- **Best pair(s):** OECD geopolitical events (elections, conflict outcomes, diplomatic negotiations) 14–45 days from resolution; Mode B: geopolitics + politics/finance 30–90 days; Avoid: sports (PM advantage), weather (ensemble-forecast-edge prim), science/AI (Metaculus speciality but no PM semantic match)
- **Best timeframe:** Mode A: 14–45 days to resolution (peak calibration-horizon zone); community-median source: 14–30 days only. Mode B: 30–90 days. Hold until convergence < 2%, resolution, or hard stop (45d Mode A / 90d Mode B). Entry at signal detection; no timing required.
- **Key numbers:** Metaculus Brier **0.111** vs PM Brier **0.187** (~40% accuracy gap); GJP vs IC prediction markets: **−30% Brier** (Tetlock 2015); performance-weighted vs unweighted aggregation: weighted outperforms CDA, unweighted ties CDA (Atanasov 2024 — operative variable); frequency: **~30–100 qualifying events/year** (derived); N=30 achievable **3–12 months**; estimated systematic competitors: **< 20 globally**; breakeven WR at 4% fee: **52%**; safe target: **57%**; realistic WR estimate: **55–65%** (hypothesis — no own-data)
- **Critical findings:** (1) **Source-quality tiering is the key upgrade**: Atanasov et al. (2024) establishes that unweighted community median only *ties* prediction markets — flat 8% threshold to community median overestimates edge; 12% threshold required. (2) **Peaked calibration window**: Wiley (2012) + Mellers mechanism decomposition establish < 7d PM advantage and ~14–45d peak Metaculus advantage — the horizon is not a simple floor but a curve. (3) **Slow-edge moat quantified**: execution-irrelevant at 7–45d holds; < 20 systematic competitors; semantic matching is the entry barrier, not latency. (4) **N=30 in 3–12 months**: most statistically tractable PM information-edge prim in bank (vs financial-market-lead-lag's 4–8 years). (5) **No direct WR study exists** — no peer-reviewed paper tests "Metaculus divergence ≥ 8% → PM resolution direction WR"; hypothesis ceiling is permanent until own-data reaches N=30.
- **Evidence:** 8 sources — Tetlock & Mellers 2015 Psych Sci, Mellers et al. 2015 Psych Sci, Manifund/Medium Brier comparison, **Atanasov et al. SSRN 4691513 2024 (performance-weighted aggregation > CDA — key new source for tiering)**, Wiley 2012 Economic Journal (< 7d PM advantage), Good Judgment 2024 (FOMC), Reichenbach & Walther SSRN 5910522, Snowberg & Wolfers 2004 JEP (sequential co-pricing)
- **Implementation gaps (5):** (1) `src/classifiers/metaculus_pm_matcher.py` [BLOCKING ALL TRADES — sentence-transformer ≥ 0.85 + source-quality tier classifier]; (2) `src/signals/metaculus_feed.py` — tracked-forecaster subset API; (3) `src/strategies/superforecaster_lead.py` — 3-tier threshold table + peaked horizon window; (4) `src/risk/antiprims.py` — 3 formal escape hatch circuit-breakers; (5) tracked-forecaster quality proxy (Metaculus `track_record` field or 200-predictor fallback)
- **Last validated:** never (cycle 41 RESEARCH; sophisticated elevation; 3 core upgrades: source-quality tiering, peaked calibration window, frequency model; 8-source basis; no direct WR study — hypothesis ceiling permanently acknowledged; THREE anti-prim: (A) rolling-20 WR < 55%; (B) 30-trade geopolitics WR < 52%; (C) < 10 events/year 2 consecutive years)

## favourite-longshot-bias-fade (naive) — 2026-04-11 [historical — superseded by intermediate 2026-04-11]
- **Works when:** YES < 0.07 (buy NO) or YES > 0.93 (buy YES); geopolitics (0% fee) or politics/finance (4% fee, near-zero at extremes); liquidity ≥ $5k; bid-ask ≤ $0.05; resolution 3–90 days; no breaking news within 24h; not live-score-dependent sports market; market not in resolution-convergence phase (price not already moving to 0/1 due to imminent resolution); market ≥ 48h old (past discovery phase)
- **Fails when:** Fat-tail genuine uncertainty (**#1 failure mode**) — p=0.04 may be correctly priced for events with real black-swan resolution risk (nuclear escalation, assassination, extreme weather, existential events); oracle ambiguity at extremes — near-zero-probability YES resolutions often driven by edge-case oracle interpretation, not the named event; sports live scoring (in-game longshot pricing reflects real-time probability, not bias); crypto category (7.2% fee compresses net edge even at extremes); thin liquidity < $5k (single large order manufactures or destroys apparent bias); newly listed market (< 48h — price discovery not complete); price recently moved sharply toward extreme from ≥ 0.15 (active information event underway)
- **Best pair(s):** Geopolitical event markets (0% fee = pure behavioural-bias edge); political binary markets (4% fee, near-zero at extremes)
- **Best timeframe:** Hold to resolution; 3–90 day horizon; no active management required; no timing skill needed
- **Key numbers:** Probability weighting function γ ≈ 0.65 (Kahneman & Tversky 1979): at p=0.05, w(0.05) ≈ 0.13 — **overweighted 2.6×**; at p=0.95, w(0.95) ≈ 0.87 — underweighted; horse racing FLB magnitude 2–5% at p < 0.10 (Snowberg & Wolfers 2010); Polymarket-specific magnitude **unknown** (core evidence gap); fee at extremes: 0.04 × 0.05 × 0.95 = **0.0019 per $1 contract** (0.2%) — near-zero transaction cost
- **Critical findings:** (1) **No external data feed required** — the price level itself is the signal; works universally across all PM categories including those not on Metaculus/Kalshi. (2) **Structurally distinct from all 9 prior prim classes** — the only prim exploiting intrinsic crowd probability weighting bias. (3) **Fee advantage**: extreme-probability zone has near-zero transaction costs, making modest calibration edges viable. (4) **Competing sophistication**: FLB is well-documented in academic literature — institutional PM participants have partially arbitraged this; residual may be smaller than theoretical. (5) **Fat-tail classifier gap**: no automatic method to separate "overpriced by bias" from "fairly priced for tail event" — manual market-type review and category blocklist are the only mitigants. (6) **Polymarket-specific magnitude unknown** — horse racing 2–5% may not transfer to PM (more sophisticated participants, financial incentives). Own-data is the only resolution.
- **Evidence:** 6 sources — Kahneman & Tversky (1979, Econometrica), Snowberg & Wolfers (2010, AER), Wolfers & Zitzewitz (2004, JEP), Ottaviani & Sørensen (2008, JEcon Theory), Manski (2006, JFE), Reichenbach & Walther (SSRN 5910522); zero own-data trades
- **Implementation gaps:** (1) `src/strategies/longshot_fade.py` — new strategy file; (2) extreme-probability market scanner; (3) fat-tail category keyword blocklist (nuclear/war-escalation/assassination/extreme-natural-disaster); (4) resolution horizon filter; (5) Polymarket calibration scan from Gamma API historical data to validate magnitude
- **Last validated:** never (NEW naive prim, cycle 43; 10th polymarket prim class; behavioural-bias mechanism — unique among all PM prims; 6-source academic basis; Polymarket-specific magnitude untested; fat-tail genuine uncertainty = #1 failure mode; refinement path: Gamma API calibration scan → fat-tail classifier → threshold plateau test)

## favourite-longshot-bias-fade (intermediate) — 2026-04-11 [historical — superseded by sophisticated 2026-04-11]
- **Works when — Mode A (sharp tail):** YES < 0.05 OR YES > 0.95; geopolitics category ONLY (0% fee); liquidity ≥ $5k; bid-ask ≤ $0.04; market age ≥ 48h; resolution horizon 7–90 days; event type = W (non-fat-tail); no breaking news within 24h; market not in resolution-convergence phase
- **Works when — Mode B (soft tail):** YES 0.05–0.07 OR YES 0.93–0.95; geopolitics category ONLY (0% fee); liquidity ≥ $10k; bid-ask ≤ $0.03; market age ≥ 72h; resolution horizon 14–90 days; event type = W; no breaking news within 24h; not in resolution-convergence phase
- **Fails when:** Event type X (nuclear/war declaration/assassination/sovereign default/government collapse/extinction/coup/martial law/impeachment/constitutional crisis/biological attack/cyberattack grid) — skip unconditionally; event type Y (oracle-ambiguous: consecutive, exceeding, equivalent to, continuous, at least, minimum of, >24h thresholds) — skip; event type Z (sports live scoring: category=sports and resolution within 4h) — skip; politics/finance/crypto categories — Mode B fee drag (0.24%) compresses net edge below viability floor; thin liquidity < $5k Mode A / < $10k Mode B; active information event underway (price moved from ≥ 0.15 toward extreme within prior 24h); market age < 48h Mode A / < 72h Mode B (discovery phase incomplete)
- **Best pair(s):** Geopolitics event markets (0% fee = pure behavioural-bias edge; Mode A net edge 1.0–1.7pp after discount; Mode B net edge 0.6–1.1pp)
- **Best timeframe:** Hold to resolution; Mode A 7–90d; Mode B 14–90d; no active management required
- **Key numbers:** Manifold Markets calibration (1M+ markets, PM-adjacent): p=5% resolves at ~7.5% (+2.5pp bias); p=3% resolves at ~4.5% (+1.5pp); PM-to-Manifold discount 60–70% (financial incentives); Mode A net edge estimate 1.0–1.7pp (geopolitics, 0% fee); Mode B net edge estimate 0.6–1.1pp; Kahneman-Tversky γ ≈ 0.65: w(0.05) ≈ 0.128 (2.6× overweighted); fee at p=0.05 geopolitics: $0 (0% category)
- **Critical findings:** (1) **Mechanism resolved to misperception model** (Snowberg & Wolfers 2010 AER) — NOT risk-love preferences; implication: structurally persistent edge that will not be fully arbitraged away even with rational capital entry. (2) **Thin-market persistence** (Ottaviani & Sørensen 2008): rational capital avoids extreme-probability zones because absolute dollar return per contract is too small to justify execution cost — creates stable unexploited niche even in otherwise efficient markets. (3) **Mode B geopolitics-only restriction**: at p=0.06 with 4% politics fee, drag = 0.24% cutting estimated 0.6–1.1pp FLB to 0.37–0.87pp — below the 0.5% viable floor at pessimistic end; Mode B restricted to geopolitics to eliminate this drag. (4) **Manifold magnitude anchor**: open-source PM-adjacent calibration data provides first empirical magnitude estimate; horse racing baseline (2–5%) now supplemented by prediction market-class evidence (+2.5pp at p=0.05 after discount adjustment). (5) **4-type fat-tail taxonomy** (X/Y/Z/W): deterministic keyword veto replaces manual review — eliminates the #1 failure mode ambiguity of the naive prim.
- **Evidence:** 8 sources — Kahneman & Tversky (1979, Econometrica), Snowberg & Wolfers (2010, AER — misperception resolution), Ottaviani & Sørensen (2008, JEcon Theory — thin-market persistence), Wolfers & Zitzewitz (2004, JEP), Manski (2006, JFE — Intrade calibration gap), Manifold Markets open calibration (2021–2024, 1M+ markets), Reichenbach & Walther (SSRN 5910522 — 124M PM trades), Tetlock (2005, Superforecasting — misperception persistence in calibrated forecasters); zero own-data trades
- **Anti-prim escape hatches:** (A) If Gamma API calibration scan shows < +0.5pp average FLB at p < 0.05 on resolved Polymarket markets (n ≥ 50) → mechanism weak; revert to precursor anti-prim; (B) Live WR < 52% (Mode A) or < 55% (Mode B) over first 30 trades at target thresholds → mechanism not manifesting; revert
- **Implementation gaps:** (1) `src/strategies/longshot_fade.py` with Mode A/B logic and Type X/Y/Z/W classifier; (2) Gamma API historical calibration scan (BLOCKING — validates magnitude before deployment); (3) threshold plateau test across Mode A ∈ [0.03, 0.04, 0.05] and Mode B ∈ [0.06, 0.07, 0.08]
- **Last validated:** never (cycle 44 RESEARCH; intermediate elevation from naive; misperception mechanism confirmed; two-mode threshold structure; 4-type fat-tail taxonomy; Manifold calibration as primary magnitude anchor; Mode B geopolitics-only restriction; 8-source basis; sophisticated path: Gamma API calibration scan + threshold plateau test)

- **Works when — Mode A (sharp tail):** YES ∈ [0.03, 0.05]; geopolitics category ONLY (0% fee); market age ≤ 90 days from listing (prefer ≤ 30 days — listing window); liquidity ≥ $5k; bid-ask ≤ 0.04; market age ≥ 48h; resolution 7–90 days remaining; event type = W (named binary political/geo, oracle-stable, base rate estimable, no live cascade); no breaking news within 24h
- **Works when — Mode B (soft tail):** YES ∈ (0.05, 0.07]; geopolitics ONLY; market age ≤ 90 days (prefer ≤ 30); liquidity ≥ $10k; bid-ask ≤ 0.03; market age ≥ 72h; resolution 14–90 days remaining; event type = W
- **Fails when:** Market age > 90 days from listing (residual overpricing reflects genuine private information, not FLB); event type X/Y/Z (fat-tail genuine uncertainty, oracle ambiguity, or live cascade signal); breaking news within 24h; liquidity below threshold; gap > 90s (gap stale — competitor absorption); spread above mode threshold; politics/finance category (4% fee erodes net edge below 0.5pp viable floor); Gamma API scan returns < +0.5pp empirical edge in Mode A bucket (escape hatch A); Mode A live WR < 52% over 30 trades (escape hatch B); < 8 qualifying trades/year over 2 consecutive years (escape hatch C)

- **Critical findings:** (1) **Financially-incentivised anchors close the core evidence gap** — Manski 2006 (Intrade) and Gans & Leigh 2009 (Australian political) both show 1.5–2.5pp FLB at p<0.10 in real-money markets; Polymarket Mode A net edge now empirically bounded at 1.2–1.5pp rather than theory-extrapolated. (2) **Listing window model**: narrative bias strongest days 2–30 post-listing; compresses days 30–90; below viable floor >90 days; `max_age_d = 90` is now a hard filter. ## bollinger-squeeze-breakout (naive) — 2026-04-11
- **Works when:** BBW percentile < 20th (rolling 125 bars) for ≥ 5 consecutive bars; BB fully inside Keltner Channel (squeeze active); first squeeze release bar (BBU > KCU); momentum oscillator positive at release; next-candle hold above BBM (reaction accepted); volume ≥ SMA(20) at release; BTC/ETH on 1h–4h; HTF context neutral-to-bullish
- **Fails when:** Micro-squeeze chop (≥ 3 squeeze events in 20 bars — level over-tested, mechanism absent); squeeze releases against HTF trend (bearish 4h EMA200); same-candle entry without next-candle confirmation (costs 5–10pp WR per sister prim meta-analysis); KC multiplier too tight (too many false squeezes) or too wide (too few squeezes for statistical viability); low-liquidity altcoins (BB unreliable with sparse volume); momentum at release is near zero (no directional conviction)
- **Best pair(s):** BTC/USDT, ETH/USDT (untested — hypothetical; institutional accumulation makes vol compression mechanism most plausible)
- **Best timeframe:** 4h (untested — fewer micro-squeezes than 1h; daily too rare); 1h secondary
- **Key numbers:** ~15–20% of bars in squeeze (Bollinger 2001); breakout resolves within 15 bars; practitioner WR claim 55–65% (Carter/LazyBear, methodology undisclosed); crypto-specific WR = pending backtest
- **Critical findings:** (1) Mechanism is theoretically grounded in ARCH/GARCH volatility clustering — vol mean-reverts from compressed to expanded; this is a stylized fact, not a hypothesis. (2) The rsi-oversold-mean-reversion sophisticated prim **explicitly excludes** BB squeeze → expansion as failure mode #7 — this prim is designed to cover exactly that excluded zone. (3) No peer-reviewed crypto backtest exists; Carter/LazyBear claims are practitioner-level with selection bias risk. (4) Momentum histogram direction at release is the critical empirical question — whether it reliably identifies accumulation direction on crypto is entirely untested. (5) Signal frequency unknown — if < 10 qualifying squeezes/year on BTC/ETH 4h, statistical validation blocked (requires 5+ years).
- **8th regime axis:** Vol compression → expansion; distinct from all 7 existing prims (oscillator exhaustion, stop sweep, trend re-entry, two-pivot divergence, panic capitulation, derivatives crowding, anti-prim hidden-div).
- **Implementation gaps:** (1) No current strategy home — needs new signal tier; (2) squeeze duration plateau test [3,5,8,10,15] bars; (3) KC scalar plateau test [1.5, 2.0]; (4) BBW percentile plateau test [15, 20, 25]; (5) next-candle confirmation (entry on release.shift(1)); (6) volume filter at release (≥ 1.0× SMA20); (7) CVD directional filter (confirm buyers absorbed squeeze); (8) 30-cell parameter grid; DSR correction mandatory.
- **Last validated:** never (NEW naive prim, cycle 42; 8th freqtrade mechanism class; vol compression → expansion fills the regime gap explicitly excluded by rsi-oversold-mean-reversion sophisticated prim)

## bollinger-squeeze-breakout (intermediate) — 2026-04-11 [historical — superseded by sophisticated 2026-04-11]
- **Works when:** BBW percentile < 20th (rolling 125 bars) for ≥ 8 consecutive bars; BB fully inside Keltner Channel (kc_scalar 1.5–2.0, plateau-verified); 4h EMA200 slope positive (ema_200 > ema_200.shift(20)); ADX_4h < 35 (not parabolic); release bar: close > open (bullish body) AND momentum > 0 AND CVD net positive over last 3 bars AND volume ≥ 1.5× SMA(20); next-candle structure break (close > release_candle_high.shift(1) AND close > bbm.shift(1)); BTC/ETH 4h primary
- **Fails when:** Duration < 5 bars (micro-squeeze — insufficient trapped-cohort build); 4h EMA200 slope negative (macro downtrend — release enters seller supply zone, not trapped-cohort unwind); ADX_4h > 35 (parabolic regime — squeeze resolves as trend acceleration, not trapped-cohort unwind); release volume < 1.5× SMA (mechanical, no institutional absorption); CVD net negative pre-release (directionally ambiguous — possible short squeeze from bearish accumulation, not buyer accumulation); release candle bearish (close < open, body conflicts despite positive momentum oscillator); repeated micro-squeezes (≥ 3 in 20 bars — level degrading); altcoins with sparse volume (BB unreliable, CVD meaningless); same-candle entry (costs 5–10pp WR per sister prim meta); kc_scalar miscalibrated
- **Best pair(s):** BTC/USDT, ETH/USDT (frequency count: 23+23=46 entries / 3yr at naive parameters; estimated 9–10/year at ≥ 8 bar threshold — above 8/year viability floor)
- **Best timeframe:** 4h (GARCH persistence on crypto means compressions last longer and end more violently than equities; 1h = excess micro-squeezes, fee drag lethal per BSIC ~47% Sharpe erosion)
- **Key numbers:** ≥ 8 bar threshold removes ~40% of naive signals (estimated 9–10/year combined at intermediate parameters); GARCH α+β = 0.968 (Katsiampa 2017 — near unit-root vs equities ~0.92); equity baseline WR 52–55% (no confirmation); with full gate stack: 55–62% hypothesis; OOS degradation expected 25–50%; fee Sharpe erosion ~47%; statistical validation floor n=30 (~3 years at intermediate frequency)
- **Critical findings:** (1) Katsiampa (2017) GARCH(1,1) on Bitcoin: α+β = 0.968 (near unit-root) — crypto volatility compressions stickier and expansions more violent than equities; this is the mechanism amplifier distinguishing crypto from equity implementations. (2) Duration-magnitude correlation (Connors 2009): each additional compression bar adds a trapped-agent cohort; ≥ 8 bars = larger stop cascade at release. (3) 4h EMA200 slope is a mechanism gate, not a refinement — downtrend squeeze releases enter seller supply (different agent configuration: shorts riding trend, longs trapped), not the compression-to-expansion regime. (4) CVD 3-bar pre-release resolves the naive "momentum may be noise" limitation: negative CVD pre-release flags possible short squeeze (bearish accumulation) rather than buyer accumulation — directionally ambiguous setup. (5) Structure break confirmation (close > release high, not just BBM hold) required: BBM hold is a continuation-bias entry; structure break is an acceptance entry.
- **Implementation gaps (7):** (1) duration_min 5 → 8; (2) add close > open to entry_signal; (3) add CVD 3-bar rolling sum > 0; (4) volume threshold 1.0× → 1.5×; (5) add 4h EMA200 slope informative pair; (6) add ADX_4h < 35 gate; (7) next-candle confirmation: close > release_high.shift(1) AND close > bbm.shift(1)
- **Plateau grid:** 45 cells — duration ∈ [5,8,10,12,15] × kc_scalar ∈ [1.5,1.75,2.0] × bbw_pctl ∈ [0.15,0.20,0.25]; CPCV + DSR mandatory (> 20 cells); PF variance < 25% across stable plateau = sophisticated promotion criterion
- **Anti-prim escape hatches:** (A) If actual signal count < 15 in 3yr at ≥ 8 bar threshold → frequency anti-prim; relax to ≥ 5 and re-evaluate. (B) No cell WR > 52% across 45-cell grid → mechanism not producing edge on crypto 4h → anti-prim
- **Last validated:** never (cycle 46 RESEARCH; intermediate elevation from naive; upgrades: GARCH persistence anchor [Katsiampa 2017], 8-bar duration threshold, body direction + CVD + 1.5× volume gate, 4h EMA200 slope mechanism gate, ADX_4h < 35, structure break confirmation; 8-source basis; 45-cell plateau test is BLOCKING pre-deployment gate)

- **Works when:** BBW percentile < 20th (rolling 125 bars) for ≥ 8 consecutive bars; BB fully inside Keltner Channel (kc_scalar 1.5–2.0, plateau-verified); 4h EMA200 slope positive (ema_200 > ema_200.shift(20)); ADX_4h < 35 (not parabolic); release bar: close > open (bullish body) AND momentum > 0 AND CVD net positive over last 3 bars AND volume ≥ 1.5× SMA(20); next-candle structure break (close > release_candle_high.shift(1) AND close > bbm.shift(1)); fee-adjusted R:R ≥ 1:2 (stop below squeeze range low); parameter plateau verified (PF variance < 25% across 45-cell grid); CPCV+DSR correction applied; BTC/ETH 4h primary

- **Key numbers:** GARCH α+β = 0.968 (Katsiampa 2017 — near unit-root, vs equities ~0.92); σ_conditional/σ_LR = 2.11× at K=8 bars (Bitcoin) vs 1.43× (equity) = 1.48× relative amplification; Connors & Alvarez N-day lowest-ATR stocks WR 67.3% (5d forward); WR ladder: bare release ~50% → equity confluence full stack ~65-67% → crypto realistic 52-58% → live post-OOS 48-52%; IS WR ≥ 55% required for live ≥ 48% after OOS degradation (McLean-Pontiff 25-50%); fee Sharpe erosion ~47% (BSIC); signal frequency post-filters ~4-9/year (anti-prim A proximity); n_eff=30 requires ~4-6 years at current frequency; 45-cell plateau + CPCV+DSR mandatory (>20-cell PBO threshold breached)

(5) N_eff framework: BTC/ETH ρ≈0.70 → N_eff ≈ 1.3/signal; statistical floor n_eff=30 requires ~4-6 years of live deployment at this frequency — not achievable with backtest window alone. (6) PBO threshold: 45-cell grid exceeds 20-cell PBO boundary → raw Sharpe MUST NOT be reported; CPCV+DSR are non-optional.

## no-event-time-decay-fade (naive) — 2026-04-11 [historical — superseded by intermediate, cycle 49]
- **Works when:** Binary PM contract pricing an event that can be modelled as a Poisson arrival process (political/diplomatic/legislative actions with stable historical base rates); YES price 0.05–0.35; days_remaining ≤ 21; YES > actuarial_yes(λ_cat, days_remaining) × 1.30; no positive YES catalyst in prior 72h; liquidity ≥ $5k; bid-ask spread ≤ 0.04
- **Fails when:** Event is NOT Poisson (breaking news cascade; live crisis with real-time informed trading; scheduled legislative vote with known date — geometric-decay model breaks down); λ estimate is wrong for the category (most dangerous failure mode — miscalibrated λ makes every signal noise); YES already at extreme tail < 0.07 (FLB prim is the correct mechanism there); oracle/resolution ambiguity; YES has already partially decayed (signal has fired too late, actuarial overpricing compressed to < 1.30×); bursty political news cycle (Poisson independence assumption violated); market with < $5k liquidity (spread eat any edge)
- **Best pair(s):** Geopolitics, Politics, Macro event markets on Polymarket — NOT crypto price markets, NOT sports
- **Best timeframe:** Hold to resolution; entry in final ≤ 21 days; no active management (hold until resolution or catalyst invalidation)
- **Key numbers:** Signal threshold: YES / actuarial_fair ≥ 1.30 AND days_remaining ≤ 21; CATEGORY_LAMBDA lookup table (initial estimates — not yet calibrated): geopolitics_military 0.003/day, legislative_passage 0.005/day, executive_action 0.008/day, diplomatic_agreement 0.002/day; Kahneman & Tversky 1974 anchoring: subjects adjust insufficiently from anchor — YES listing price is the anchor; Moontower APY analysis: negative APY/time-to-resolution relationship empirically observed; Black-Scholes binary option theta: significant and non-linear near expiry; α=0.10 Kelly
- **Critical findings:** (1) This is the 11th Polymarket prim class — mechanism is temporal probability trajectory divergence, NOT static probability misperception (FLB). The two activate in non-overlapping regions: FLB fires at YES < 0.07 any horizon; time-decay fires at YES 0.05–0.35 when actuarial ratio ≥ 1.30 in the final 21 days. (2) λ estimation is the blocking problem — wrong λ inverts the signal. Initial lookup table is first-principles derived; must be validated against resolved PM markets before deployment. (3) Poisson assumption is a first-order approximation — political events are not fully memoryless (news cycles cluster). The signal should be suspended when a relevant news cluster is active. (4) The Moontower APY fingerprint is the strongest empirical support: the observed negative-APY/time-to-resolution pattern in PM contracts is exactly what Poisson-decay-anchoring predicts. (5) Distinct conceptually from FLB: FLB is about the shape of the probability weighting function (static overpricing at extreme p); time-decay-fade is about the failure to update the probability downward as T decreases — a dynamic process, not a static distortion.
- **Evidence:** Kahneman & Tversky (1974, Science — anchoring and adjustment), Manski (2006, JFE — Intrade probability persistence), Moontower APY/time-to-resolution empirical fingerprint, Black-Scholes binary option theta (theoretical), diSessa/Louca epistemic cognition (knowledge evolution framework); no own-data trades
- **Anti-prim escape hatches:** (A) Gamma API calibration scan returns overpricing_ratio < 1.30 in all three YES buckets (0.05–0.35, final 14d) with n ≥ 20/bucket — mechanism not present at calibratable magnitude → retire. (B) λ lookup table calibrated against resolved markets shows predicted actuarial_yes systematically LOWER than empirical resolution frequency in any category → λ estimate is wrong, not the market → recalibrate before deployment. (C) Live NO positions on this signal lose > 60% over first 10 trades → stop, run escape hatch A scan, reassess λ estimates.
- **Implementation gaps:** (1) `src/strategies/time_decay_fade.py` — not yet created; skeleton in prim file; (2) `CATEGORY_LAMBDA` lookup table requires empirical calibration against resolved PM markets (BLOCKING pre-deployment); (3) Gamma API calibration scan (`gamma_calibration_scan()`) — BLOCKING pre-deployment; (4) Catalyst monitoring (72h no-catalyst check) requires NLP news feed or manual monitoring; (5) λ per-market override mechanism for scheduled events (legislative calendar, FOMC dates — these are NOT Poisson); (6) Integration with fractional-kelly-sizing sophisticated (α=0.10 until calibration confirms higher α is justified)
- **Last validated:** never (NEW naive prim, cycle 48 RESEARCH; 11th Polymarket prim class; mechanism: Poisson actuarial decay + anchoring bias; distinct from FLB [temporal trajectory vs static distortion]; two academic anchors: Kahneman-Tversky 1974 + Manski 2006 + Moontower empirical fingerprint; BLOCKING: λ calibration + Gamma API scan before any deployment)
