## sequential-contrast-effect-signal (naive — polymarket) — 2026-04-13 [cycle 136]
- **Works when:** High-salience anchor event E_A resolved in past 7 days (GDELT article_count_48h > P90_category; resolved against PM consensus ≥ 55%); distinct minor concurrent event E_M in same category C (GDELT article_count_48h ∈ [P40, P75] for that category); E_M YES ∈ [0.12, 0.65]; E_M YES < category_base_rate − 10 pp; entity_sim(E_A, E_M) < 0.70; E_M resolution > 14 days; liquidity ≥ $5k; bid-ask ≤ $0.05; ACLED delta < +20% and NOAA < MODERATE at E_M location (stable conditions guard); anchor E_A GDELT velocity declining (peak passed)
- **Fails when:** E_A provides genuine probability-REDUCING information about E_M (e.g., peace agreement reduces conflict E_M probability — confound dominates); E_M and E_A are sequential cascade stages (use conditional-probability-cascade-arbitrage instead); E_A still rising in salience (anchor not yet established); category base rate database unavailable (BLOCKING — shared with CBRNF); GDELT category baselines unavailable (BLOCKING — shared with AERB-fade); E_M in FLB zone (YES < 0.10 → defer to FLB prim); ACLED/NOAA deterioration at E_M location (genuine information update, not contrast bias); E_A was TYPICAL rather than OUTLIER (assimilation replaces contrast; P90 gate biases toward outlier but boundary not sharp)
- **Mechanism:** Contrast effect in sequential probability judgment (Parducci 1965 range-frequency theory; Tversky & Simonson 1993 contrast in choice). E_A sets new dramatic range maximum for category C; E_M appears "small" relative to this ceiling; PM participants deflate P(E_M) below actuarial base rate through 3 sub-mechanisms: (1) range-frequency recalibration; (2) attention diversion to E_A aftermath; (3) coherent arbitrariness (Ariely et al. 2003) — E_M priced to appear coherently less dramatic than E_A. DEFLATION counterpart to AERB-fade's INFLATION mechanism. Both triggered by same E_A but predict opposite directions on different markets.
- **Directional relationship to AERB-fade:** AERB-fade: E_A → BUY NO on other over-inflated category markets (availability inflation). Sequential-contrast: E_A → BUY YES on concurrent E_M under-deflated market (contrast deflation). Both can fire simultaneously from same E_A; targets are different markets; N_eff correlation ρ ≈ 0.30 when co-active (shared trigger, different targets).
- **Key numbers:** Anchor window 7 days; contrast gap threshold 10 pp below base rate; anchor window GDELT P90 (category-specific); E_M salience range P40–P75; entity_sim cutoff 0.70; α=0.10 (mandatory floor); max hold 14 days; estimated 8–20 qualifying pairs/year
- **Evidence:** 6 academic anchors — Parducci (1965 PsychRev: range-frequency theory); Tversky & Simonson (1993 PsychRev: contrast effects in choice); Schwarz & Bless (1992 PSPB: outlier exemplars trigger contrast not assimilation); Johnson & Tversky (1983 JPSP: cross-category salience contamination); Hogarth & Einhorn (1992 PsychRev: belief-adjustment model, sequential anchor underadjustment); Ariely/Loewenstein/Prelec (2003 QJE: coherent arbitrariness)
- **Blocking gates:** category_base_rates.py (shared with CBRNF — BLOCKING); GDELT P40/P75/P90 category baselines (shared with AERB-fade — BLOCKING); category_tagger.py NLP classifier (shared); entity_sim.py (shared with AERB-fade)
- **Last validated:** never (RESEARCH creation — cycle 136; 23rd polymarket signal class; contrast-deflation mechanism distinct from AERB-fade availability-inflation; all infrastructure BLOCKING; zero own-data; DRY_RUN)
- **Prim bank after cycle 136:** polymarket **22 naive** (+1: sequential-contrast-effect) / 22 intermediate / 23 sophisticated

## sequential-contrast-effect-signal (intermediate — polymarket) — 2026-04-13 [cycle 143]
- **Works when (Mode A — Natural Disaster):** HIGH-tier E_A (GDELT count > P90_natural_disaster ~1,200/48h; salience_ratio ≥ 1.0); anchor resolved against PM consensus ≥ 55% (surprise gate); E_M in same `natural_disaster` category (GDELT P40–P75 salience); E_M YES < category_base_rate − 10pp; entity_sim(E_A, E_M) < 0.70; geographic distance > 500km; NOAA < MODERATE + USGS < 4.5 at E_M location (72h); anchor GDELT velocity declining (< 0.7× peak); AERB-fade NOT firing BUY NO on E_M (contrast mechanism confirmed); E_M resolution > 14d; liquidity ≥ $5k; bid-ask ≤ $0.05; G3 cleared (WR ≥ 60% at N ≥ 20) — BLOCKING for live
- **Works when (Mode B — Political Violence / Electoral):** Same structure; ACLED delta at E_M region < +20% (7d post-anchor vs 30d baseline); distinct primary_actor AND conflict_system_id from E_A; E_M resolution > 21d; 0.75× Mode A size; α=0.08
- **Fails when:** Anti-prim A: IS scan WR < 50% at N ≥ 20 → retire mode (mechanism absent or confound dominant); Anti-prim B: anchor salience_ratio < 1.0 (fails P90 threshold) → skip; Anti-prim C: ACLED delta ≥ +20% at E_M region (Mode B) → genuine escalation confound → skip; AERB-fade fires BUY NO on E_M from same E_A → availability dominates → AVAILABILITY gate fires → skip sequential-contrast on E_M; E_A still rising in salience (velocity ≥ 0.7× peak) → anchor not yet established → wait; E_M is cascade stage of E_A → use conditional-probability-cascade-arbitrage; category base rate database unavailable → BLOCKING; GDELT P40/P75/P90 baselines unavailable → BLOCKING; E_M YES < 0.10 (FLB zone) → defer to favourite-longshot-bias-fade; Anti-prim D: AERB-fade / sequential-contrast co-fire on same E_M in opposite directions > 20% of N ≥ 30 pair evaluations → direction classifier partition broken → rebuild with salience-tier hard separation
- **Mechanism:** Parducci (1965) range-frequency theory — E_A expands the upper bound of the experiential range for category C; E_M (moderate salience) is deflated relative to this new ceiling; PM participants estimate P(E_M) by where E_M falls in the recalibrated range → below actuarial base rate. Contrast vs assimilation direction classifier (Schwarz & Bless 1992): HIGH-tier E_A (salience_ratio ≥ 1.5) = outlier → contrast dominant; MODERATE-tier (1.0–1.5) = boundary zone → 0.5× scalar + AERB-fade silence confirmation; LOW-tier → skip. AERB-fade silence on E_M is the positive contrast indicator: AERB-fade fires BUY NO when E_M is over-priced (availability inflation); when AERB-fade is silent, E_M has not been inflated → is possibly deflated by contrast. The two prims are mutually exclusive predictions on the same E_M market.
- **Three intermediate upgrades over naive:** (1) Two-mode condition-stability structure (Mode A: NOAA/USGS stability gates + geographic distance gate; Mode B: ACLED delta + distinct-trigger gate); (2) Contrast vs availability direction classifier formalised (AERB-fade silence = contrast confirmed; HIGH-tier anchor = full position; MODERATE-tier = 0.5× + AERB silence required); (3) IS backtest protocol specified (GDELT 2022–2026 + PM Gamma API; N ≥ 20 per mode; WR ≥ 60% pass; Mann-Whitney p < 0.10)
- **New academic anchors at intermediate:** Mussweiler & Strack (2000 JPSP) — selective accessibility model: extreme standards (P90 outliers) activate knowledge inconsistent with target → contrast; grounds why P90 is the assimilation/contrast boundary; Kahneman & Miller (1986 PsychRev) — norm theory: E_A becomes the category norm against which E_M is assessed; explains why contrast deflation persists while E_A is recent; grounds 7-day anchor window and velocity decay exit
- **Key numbers:** Anchor window 7 days; contrast gap 10pp below base rate; P90 anchor threshold (category-specific); E_M salience P40–P75; entity_sim cutoff 0.70; salience_ratio HIGH ≥ 1.5 / MODERATE 1.0–1.5; α=0.10 Mode A / α=0.08 Mode B; 0.75× size Mode B; 0.5× scalar MODERATE-tier; max hold 14d Mode A / 10d Mode B; N_eff ρ=0.30 when co-active with AERB-fade on different E_M markets
- **Infrastructure BLOCKING (all shared):** category_base_rates.py (shared with CBRNF); GDELT P40/P75/P90 category baselines (shared with AERB-fade); category_tagger.py NLP classifier (shared); entity_sim.py (shared with AERB-fade); AERB-fade signal monitor (new dependency — contrast_vs_availability_gate requires querying live AERB-fade signal state)
- **Last validated:** never (RESEARCH elevation — cycle 143; naive cycle 136 → intermediate cycle 143; 3 structural upgrades; 8 academic anchors; 3 formal anti-prims + anti-prim D; all infrastructure BLOCKING; G3 not yet executed; N=0 own-data; DRY_RUN)

- **Blocking gates:** G1_24A (≥ 10 sustained ABSORPTION episodes/year on live data, 7d separation); G1_24B (WR(next-4h BTC > 0 | ABSORPTION) ≥ 53%, n ≥ 10); G1_24C (3-poll WR > single-poll WR — spoofing filter adds value); G1_24D (ADX regime gate improves WR by ≥ 2pp); INDEP_24 (ρ < 0.60 for axes 6/7/11/13)
- **Anti-prim gates:** A (< 8 ABSORPTION episodes/year → retire: signal too ephemeral at 15m poll); B (WR ≤ 0.50 or wrong direction → retire: no next-bar predictive content); C (ρ ≥ 0.60 vs axis 6 VWAP → merge into axis 6); D (3-poll WR ≤ single-poll WR → remove sustained gate, simplify to single-poll)
- **Best pair:** BTC/USDT spot primary (public Binance REST — no API key required)
- **Best timeframe:** 15m poll cadence; signal relevant to 1h–4h strategy entries; no 4h+ utility (book state too stale)
- **Implementation:** New strategy file YujiOrderBookDepthStrategy.py; bot_loop_start() with 15m cooldown timer; depth_ratio_history deque(maxlen=3); `order_book_weight` scalar broadcast to sister prims
- **Last validated:** never (RESEARCH creation — cycle 151; 24th freqtrade regime axis; first microstructure signal class; all gates uncleared; DRY_RUN; no API key required — G1 scan feasible immediately)
- **Prim bank after cycle 151:** freqtrade **23 naive** (+1: order-book-depth-imbalance) / 26 intermediate / 26 sophisticated

## order-book-depth-imbalance (intermediate — freqtrade axis 24) — 2026-04-13 [cycle 152]
- **Works when:** Binance BTC/USDT:USDT order book REST polling at 15min cadence detects Mode A spike (ratio > 2.0, onset from < 1.6) OR Mode B sustained imbalance (mean > 1.5, all 6 polls > 1.3) → AMPLIFY 1.08× (Mode A) / 1.05× (Mode B); inverse thresholds → SUPPRESS 0.92×/0.95×. ADX_1h < 15 (ranging) escalates Mode A weight × 1.12; ADX > 25 (trending) decays × 0.85 (Cartea 2015: imbalance in ranging market = prime signal window). VWAP anchor gate: if price >0.5% beyond VWAP_4h in signal direction → 0.50× delta decay (move already reflected in book).
- **Fails when:** FM1 spoof cascade (large order placed/cancelled within 1 poll — mitigated by pending-state reversion check); FM2 macro override (cross-asset risk-off: ETH-BTC extreme ratio change dominates microstructure); FM3 stale book (REST round-trip >45s — timestamp validation discards); FM4 funding dislocation (perp-spot basis extreme: axis 13 output < 0.90 → disable); FM5 thin-book regime (total depth < $5M — neutral returned)
- **Mode architecture:** Mode A (spike, 3-poll): onset detection (ratio crosses threshold from prior state); spoof filter: if ratio reverts fully within 1 poll → suppress, log FM1; 1–2 candle duration expectation. Mode B (sustained, 6-poll): structural repositioning / slow-flow; 3–6 candle duration. ADX conditioning inverted for Mode B (sustained more meaningful in trending context).
- **Scalar outputs:** Mode A long 1.08×; Mode A short 0.92×; Mode B long 1.05×; Mode B short 0.95×; neutral/spoof/pending 1.00×. Post-ADX-conditioning and post-VWAP-decay applied before broadcast.
- **Gate status:** G_DATA_24 ✓ (public Binance REST /depth, no API key); G1_24A/B/LAG ANALYTICALLY PRE-CONFIRMED (empirical UNCLEARED); G2_24 UNCLEARED (Mann-Whitney p<0.10 on reconstructed LOB snapshots); INDEP_24 UNCLEARED (ρ<0.70 vs axes 3/9/11); CPCV+DSR BELOW TRIGGER (8-cell grid; triggers if ADX conditioning grid-searched, pushing to ~40 cells)
- **Anti-prim escape hatches:** AP1 (5 symmetric events/30d → G1_24A re-scan; if fails, demote to naive); AP2 (3 inverse direction failures/30d → RSI_15m>45 gate activated for Mode A long); AP3 (Coinbase divergence >60% of Mode A events → cross-exchange confirmation layer deactivated, Binance-only operation)
- **N_eff independence requirement:** ρ<0.70 vs axes 3 (liquidity sweep), 9 (FVG), 11 (OI divergence) — mechanistically distinct (microstructure vs price-action vs flow) but empirical test UNCLEARED. If ρ>0.50 with any of {3,9,11}: N_eff correction applies; combined multiplier cap 1.25×.
- **IS backtest protocol:** Reconstructed Binance LOB snapshots at 15min intervals, BTC/USDT:USDT, Jan 2022 – Dec 2024; Mode A/B classification applied; signal-on vs signal-off Mann-Whitney U (p<0.10, n≥50/mode/direction); OOS holdout 2025 walk-forward 12m; DSR gate (Bailey-Borwein-Lopez de Prado SSRN 2326253) required if ADX conditioning grid-searched
- **Intermediate upgrades over naive (cycle 151):** Two-mode architecture (spike + sustained); onset-detection onset filter; spoof-filter state machine; VWAP_4h structural anchor gate with distance-based decay; ADX regime conditioning (Mode A ranging-prime / Mode B trending-prime); 5 failure modes taxonomy (FM1–FM5); 3 anti-prim escape hatches (AP1–AP3); IS backtest protocol with DSR gate; cross-exchange confirmation layer specified (Coinbase/OKX, optional, auth-dependent)
- **Implementation:** YujiOrderBookDepthStrategy.py upgraded; `_ob_data` per-symbol dict with `history: deque(maxlen=6)`, `pending_mode_a: Optional[tuple]`, `last_poll: float`; `get_order_book_weight()` callable by sister prims; `record_ap1_event()`, `record_ap2_event()`, `deactivate_cross_exchange_layer()` for escape hatch management; VWAP_4h from 4h informative pair; ADX_1h from 1h OHLCV
